# Skills · B · part 89

901 entries, brainstorm to brainstorming. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **brainstorm**: Brainstorming and idea-evaluation partner for the ClearContract project (quoting/invoicing app for tradespeople). Use whenever the user wants to discuss a new feature or product idea, question an architecture/stack/design decision, compare approaches, weigh scope trade-offs, or asks things like "what do you think about…", "should I add…", "is there a better way to…", "I have an idea…" — even casually, and even if they never say the word "brainstorm". This is a discussion skill, not an implementation skill. ([leeb48/ClearContract](https://github.com/leeb48/ClearContract/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/brainstorm))
- **brainstorm**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "brainstorm", "help me think through this", "explore ideas", "organize my thoughts", "work through this problem", or mentions needing help with complex thinking, decision-making, problem exploration, or wants a thinking partner for exploring challenges through Socratic questioning. ([lejoe/claude-plugins](https://github.com/lejoe/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorm))

- **brainstorm**: Work an idea into a decision before any code shape exists. Takes a prompt or a document describing something to build, plus its open questions, and works them one at a time - each step forks parallel sub-agents that each steward one option and must report the evidence against it, then the user decides. Every decision and the assumptions it rests on land in a durable log. Ends by handing the chosen direction to `architect`, `plan-to-issues` or `prototype`. For a grill that ends at decisions and a brief.md with no feature-spec chain, use grill-me instead. Use when the user wants to stress-test a plan, get grilled on a design, harden an idea against gaps, or says 'grill me', 'grill this', 'interrogate my plan', 'poke holes in this'. Produces N iterations (default 5) where each iteration's proposal addresses the previous iteration's adversarial review.
